## Narration 1390

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1405)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: ابْنَ شِهَابٍ (successor). The Arabic source supplies an explicitly abbreviated Ibn Shihab response. Its missing wording is not expanded from another field; compiler/Quran exposition remains context.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ ابْنَ شِهَابٍ، يُسْأَلُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ، فَقَالَ مِثْلَ مَا قَالَ سُلَيْمَانُ بْنُ يَسَارٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1405]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1405" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1405</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1405 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl"> قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَذَلِكَ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا وَذَلِكَ لِقَوْلِ اللَّهِ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى ‏{‏وَالَّذِينَ يَرْمُونَ الْمُحْصَنَاتِ ثُمَّ لَمْ يَأْتُوا بِأَرْبَعَةِ شُهَدَاءَ فَاجْلِدُوهُمْ ثَمَانِينَ جَلْدَةً وَلاَ تَقْبَلُوا لَهُمْ شَهَادَةً أَبَدًا وَأُولَئِكَ هُمُ الْفَاسِقُونَ * إِلاَّ الَّذِينَ تَابُوا مِنْ بَعْدِ ذَلِكَ وَأَصْلَحُوا فَإِنَّ اللَّهَ غَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ‏}‏‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَالأَمْرُ الَّذِي لاَ اخْتِلاَفَ فِيهِ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ الَّذِي يُجْلَدُ الْحَدَّ ثُمَّ تَابَ وَأَصْلَحَ تَجُوزُ شَهَادَتُهُ وَهُوَ أَحَبُّ مَا سَمِعْتُ إِلَىَّ فِي ذَلِكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1405" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1405 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सुलईमान बिन यसार और दीगर से सवाल हुआ के एक शख्श को हद-ए-क़ज़फ़ (तोहमत की सज़ा) मिली हो, तो क्या उसकी गवाही दुरुस्त है? उन्होंने कहा: हां, जब वो तौबा कर ले और उसकी तौबा की सच्चाई उसके आमाल से मालूम हो जाए। इब्न शिहाब से भी ये सवाल हुआ, उन्होंने भी ऐसा ही कहा। इमाम मालिक (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: हमारे नज़दीक यही हुक्म है, क्योंकि अल्लाह जल्ला जलालुहू ने फ़रमाया है के जो लोग नेक-बख्त बीवियों पर इल्ज़ाम लगाते हैं फिर चार गवाह नहीं लाते, उन्हें अस्सी (80) कोड़े मारो और कभी उनकी गवाही क़बूल न करो, वो ही गुनाहगार हैं। मगर जो लोग तौबा कर लें इसके बाद और नेक हो जाएं, तो बेशक अल्लाह बख्शने वाला मेहरबान है। पस जो शख्श हद-ए-क़ज़फ़ लगाया जाए, फिर तौबा कर ले और नेक हो जाए, तो उसकी गवाही दुरुस्त है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1405" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: successor report. Attribution: سلیمان بن یسار (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words. Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: ابن شہاب (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="rtl">سلیمان بن یسار وغیرہ سے سوال ہوا کہ ایک شخص کو حد قذف پڑی پھر اس کی گواہی درست ہے انہوں نے کہا ہاں جب وہ توبہ کر لے اور اس کی توبہ کی سچائی اس کے اعمال سے معلوم ہو جائے ۔</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="rtl">ابن شہاب سے بھی یہ سوال ہوا انہوں نے بھی ایسا ہی کہا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1405]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1405" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="rtl"> </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1405" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl"> کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک یہی حکم ہے کیونکہ اللہ جل جلالہ نے فرمایا جو لوگ لگاتے ہیں نیک بخت بیبیوں کو پھر چار گواہ نہیں لاتے ان کو اسی کوڑے مارو پھر کبھی ان کی گواہی قبول نہ کرو وہی گنہگار ہیں مگر جو لوگ توبہ کریں بعد اس کے اور نیک ہوجائیں تو بے شک اللہ بخشنے والا مہربان ہے پس جو شخص حد قذف لگایا جائے پھر توبہ لرے اور نیک ہوجائے اس کی گواہی درست ہے۔</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1405" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1405 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Sulaiman bin Yasar aur digar se sawal hua ke ek shakhs ko had-e-qazf (tohmat ki saza) mili ho, toh kya uski gawahi durust hai? Unhone kaha: Haan, jab woh tauba kar le aur uski tauba ki sachayi uske amaal se maloom ho jaye. Ibn Shihab se bhi ye sawal hua, unhone bhi aisa hi kaha. Imam Malik (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Hamare nazdeek yahi hukm hai, kyunke Allah Jalla Jalaluhu ne farmaya hai ke jo log nek-bakht bibiyon par ilzam lagate hain phir chaar gawah nahi laate, unhein assi (80) kode maaro aur kabhi unki gawahi qabool na karo, wohi gunahgar hain. Magar jo log tauba kar lein iske baad aur nek ho jayein, toh beshak Allah bakhshne wala meharban hai. Pas jo shakhs had-e-qazf lagaya jaye, phir tauba kar le aur nek ho jaye, toh uski gawahi durust hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1405" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1391

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1406)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ يَحْيَى قَالَ مَالِكٌ عَنْ جَعْفَرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَضَى بِالْيَمِينِ مَعَ الشَّاهِدِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1406]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1406" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said, &quot;Malik said from Jafar ibn Muhammad from his father that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, pronounced judgement on the basis of an oath with one witness</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1406]</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1407]</p>

## Narration 1393

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1408)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1408]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1408" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1408</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1408 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ أَبَا سَلَمَةَ بْنَ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، وَسُلَيْمَانَ بْنَ يَسَارٍ، سُئِلاَ هَلْ يُقْضَى بِالْيَمِينِ مَعَ الشَّاهِدِ فَقَالاَ نَعَمْ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ مَضَتِ السُّنَّةُ فِي الْقَضَاءِ بِالْيَمِينِ مَعَ الشَّاهِدِ الْوَاحِدِ يَحْلِفُ صَاحِبُ الْحَقِّ مَعَ شَاهِدِهِ وَيَسْتَحِقُّ حَقَّهُ فَإِنْ نَكَلَ وَأَبَى أَنْ يَحْلِفَ أُحْلِفَ الْمَطْلُوبُ فَإِنْ حَلَفَ سَقَطَ عَنْهُ ذَلِكَ الْحَقُّ وَإِنْ أَبَى أَنْ يَحْلِفَ ثَبَتَ عَلَيْهِ الْحَقُّ لِصَاحِبِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَإِنَّمَا يَكُونُ ذَلِكَ فِي الأَمْوَالِ خَاصَّةً وَلاَ يَقَعُ ذَلِكَ فِي شَىْءٍ مِنَ الْحُدُودِ وَلاَ فِي نِكَاحٍ وَلاَ فِي طَلاَقٍ وَلاَ فِي عَتَاقَةٍ وَلاَ فِي سَرِقَةٍ وَلاَ فِي فِرْيَةٍ فَإِنْ قَالَ قَائِلٌ فَإِنَّ الْعَتَاقَةَ مِنَ الأَمْوَالِ ‏.‏ فَقَدْ أَخْطَأَ لَيْسَ ذَلِكَ عَلَى مَا قَالَ وَلَوْ كَانَ ذَلِكَ عَلَى مَا قَالَ لَحَلَفَ الْعَبْدُ مَعَ شَاهِدِهِ إِذَا جَاءَ بِشَاهِدٍ أَنَّ سَيِّدَهُ أَعْتَقَهُ وَأَنَّ الْعَبْدَ إِذَا جَاءَ بِشَاهِدٍ عَلَى مَالٍ مِنَ الأَمْوَالِ ادَّعَاهُ حَلَفَ مَعَ شَاهِدِهِ وَاسْتَحَقَّ حَقَّهُ كَمَا يَحْلِفُ الْحُرُّ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَالسُّنَّةُ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ الْعَبْدَ إِذَا جَاءَ بِشَاهِدٍ عَلَى عَتَاقَتِهِ اسْتُحْلِفَ سَيِّدُهُ مَا أَعْتَقَهُ وَبَطَلَ ذَلِكَ عَنْهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَكَذَلِكَ السُّنَّةُ عِنْدَنَا أَيْضًا فِي الطَّلاَقِ إِذَا جَاءَتِ الْمَرْأَةُ بِشَاهِدٍ أَنَّ زَوْجَهَا طَلَّقَهَا أُحْلِفَ زَوْجُهَا مَا طَلَّقَهَا فَإِذَا حَلَفَ لَمْ يَقَعْ عَلَيْهِ الطَّلاَقُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَسُنَّةُ الطَّلاَقِ وَالْعَتَاقَةِ فِي الشَّاهِدِ الْوَاحِدِ وَاحِدَةٌ إِنَّمَا يَكُونُ الْيَمِينُ عَلَى زَوْجِ الْمَرْأَةِ وَعَلَى سَيِّدِ الْعَبْدِ وَإِنَّمَا الْعَتَاقَةُ حَدٌّ مِنَ الْحُدُودِ لاَ تَجُوزُ فِيهَا شَهَادَةُ النِّسَاءِ لأَنَّهُ إِذَا عَتَقَ الْعَبْدُ ثَبَتَتْ حُرْمَتُهُ وَوَقَعَتْ لَهُ الْحُدُودُ وَوَقَعَتْ عَلَيْهِ وَإِنْ زَنَى وَقَدْ أُحْصِنَ رُجِمَ وَإِنْ قَتَلَ الْعَبْدَ قُتِلَ بِهِ وَثَبَتَ لَهُ الْمِيرَاثُ بَيْنَهُ وَبَيْنَ مَنْ يُوَارِثُهُ فَإِنِ احْتَجَّ مُحْتَجٌّ فَقَالَ لَوْ أَنَّ رَجُلاً أَعْتَقَ عَبْدَهُ وَجَاءَ رَجُلٌ يَطْلُبُ سَيِّدَ الْعَبْدِ بِدَيْنٍ لَهُ عَلَيْهِ فَشَهِدَ لَهُ عَلَى حَقِّهِ ذَلِكَ رَجُلٌ وَامْرَأَتَانِ فَإِنَّ ذَلِكَ يُثْبِتُ الْحَقَّ عَلَى سَيِّدِ الْعَبْدِ حَتَّى تُرَدَّ بِهِ عَتَاقَتُهُ إِذَا لَمْ يَكُنْ لِسَيِّدِ الْعَبْدِ مَالٌ غَيْرُ الْعَبْدِ يُرِيدُ أَنْ يُجِيزَ بِذَلِكَ شَهَادَةَ النِّسَاءِ فِي الْعَتَاقَةِ فَإِنَّ ذَلِكَ لَيْسَ عَلَى مَا قَالَ وَإِنَّمَا مَثَلُ ذَلِكَ الرَّجُلُ يَعْتِقُ عَبْدَهُ ثُمَّ يَأْتِي طَالِبُ الْحَقِّ عَلَى سَيِّدِهِ بِشَاهِدٍ وَاحِدٍ فَيَحْلِفُ مَعَ شَاهِدِهِ ثُمَّ يَسْتَحِقُّ حَقَّهُ وَتُرَدُّ بِذَلِكَ عَتَاقَةُ الْعَبْدِ أَوْ يَأْتِي الرَّجُلُ قَدْ كَانَتْ بَيْنَهُ وَبَيْنَ سَيِّدِ الْعَبْدِ مُخَالَطَةٌ وَمُلاَبَسَةٌ فَيَزْعُمُ أَنَّ لَهُ عَلَى سَيِّدِ الْعَبْدِ مَالاً فَيُقَالُ لِسَيِّدِ الْعَبْدِ احْلِفْ مَا عَلَيْكَ مَا ادَّعَى فَإِنْ نَكَلَ وَأَبَى أَنْ يَحْلِفَ حُلِّفَ صَاحِبُ الْحَقِّ وَثَبَتَ حَقُّهُ عَلَى سَيِّدِ الْعَبْدِ فَيَكُونُ ذَلِكَ يَرُدُّ عَتَاقَةَ الْعَبْدِ إِذَا ثَبَتَ الْمَالُ عَلَى سَيِّدِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ وَكَذَلِكَ أَيْضًا الرَّجُلُ يَنْكِحُ الأَمَةَ فَتَكُونُ امْرَأَتَهُ فَيَأْتِي سَيِّدُ الأَمَةِ إِلَى الرَّجُلِ الَّذِي تَزَوَّجَهَا فَيَقُولُ ابْتَعْتَ مِنِّي جَارِيَتِي فُلاَنَةَ أَنْتَ وَفُلاَنٌ بِكَذَا وَكَذَا دِينَارًا ‏.‏ فَيُنْكِرُ ذَلِكَ زَوْجُ الأَمَةِ فَيَأْتِي سَيِّدُ الأَمَةِ بِرَجُلٍ وَامْرَأَتَيْنِ فَيَشْهَدُونَ عَلَى مَا قَالَ فَيَثْبُتُ بَيْعُهُ وَيَحِقُّ حَقُّهُ وَتَحْرُمُ الأَمَةُ عَلَى زَوْجِهَا وَيَكُونُ ذَلِكَ فِرَاقًا بَيْنَهُمَا وَشَهَادَةُ النِّسَاءِ لاَ تَجُوزُ فِي الطَّلاَقِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَمِنْ ذَلِكَ أَيْضًا الرَّجُلُ يَفْتَرِي عَلَى الرَّجُلِ الْحُرِّ فَيَقَعُ عَلَيْهِ الْحَدُّ فَيَأْتِي رَجُلٌ وَامْرَأَتَانِ فَيَشْهَدُونَ أَنَّ الَّذِي افْتُرِيَ عَلَيْهِ عَبْدٌ مَمْلُوكٌ فَيَضَعُ ذَلِكَ الْحَدَّ عَنِ الْمُفْتَرِي بَعْدَ أَنْ وَقَعَ عَلَيْهِ وَشَهَادَةُ النِّسَاءِ لاَ تَجُوزُ فِي الْفِرْيَةِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَمِمَّا يُشْبِهُ ذَلِكَ أَيْضًا مِمَّا يَفْتَرِقُ فِيهِ الْقَضَاءُ وَمَا مَضَى مِنَ السُّنَّةِ أَنَّ الْمَرْأَتَيْنِ يَشْهَدَانِ عَلَى اسْتِهْلاَلِ الصَّبِيِّ فَيَجِبُ بِذَلِكَ مِيرَاثُهُ حَتَّى يَرِثَ وَيَكُونُ مَالُهُ لِمَنْ يَرِثُهُ إِنْ مَاتَ الصَّبِيُّ وَلَيْسَ مَعَ الْمَرْأَتَيْنِ اللَّتَيْنِ شَهِدَتَا رَجُلٌ وَلاَ يَمِينٌ وَقَدْ يَكُونُ ذَلِكَ فِي الأَمْوَالِ الْعِظَامِ مِنَ الذَّهَبِ وَالْوَرِقِ وَالرِّبَاعِ وَالْحَوَائِطِ وَالرَّقِيقِ وَمَا سِوَى ذَلِكَ مِنَ الأَمْوَالِ وَلَوْ شَهِدَتِ امْرَأَتَانِ عَلَى دِرْهَمٍ وَاحِدٍ أَوْ أَقَلَّ مِنْ ذَلِكَ أَوْ أَكْثَرَ لَمْ تَقْطَعْ شَهَادَتُهُمَا شَيْئًا وَلَمْ تَجُزْ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَكُونَ مَعَهُمَا شَاهِدٌ أَوْ يَمِينٌ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَمِنَ النَّاسِ مَنْ يَقُولُ لاَ تَكُونُ الْيَمِينُ مَعَ الشَّاهِدِ الْوَاحِدِ ‏.‏ وَيَحْتَجُّ بِقَوْلِ اللَّهِ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى وَقَوْلُهُ الْحَقُّ ‏{‏وَاسْتَشْهِدُوا شَهِيدَيْنِ مِنْ رِجَالِكُمْ فَإِنْ لَمْ يَكُونَا رَجُلَيْنِ فَرَجُلٌ وَامْرَأَتَانِ مِمَّنْ تَرْضَوْنَ مِنَ الشُّهَدَاءِ‏}‏ يَقُولُ فَإِنْ لَمْ يَأْتِ بِرَجُلٍ وَامْرَأَتَيْنِ فَلاَ شَىْءَ لَهُ وَلاَ يُحَلَّفُ مَعَ شَاهِدِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَمِنَ الْحُجَّةِ عَلَى مَنْ قَالَ ذَلِكَ الْقَوْلَ أَنْ يُقَالَ لَهُ أَرَأَيْتَ لَوْ أَنَّ رَجُلاً ادَّعَى عَلَى رَجُلٍ مَالاً أَلَيْسَ يَحْلِفُ الْمَطْلُوبُ مَا ذَلِكَ الْحَقُّ عَلَيْهِ فَإِنْ حَلَفَ بَطَلَ ذَلِكَ عَنْهُ وَإِنْ نَكَلَ عَنِ الْيَمِينِ حُلِّفَ صَاحِبُ الْحَقِّ إِنَّ حَقَّهُ لَحَقٌّ ‏.‏ وَثَبَتَ حَقُّهُ عَلَى صَاحِبِهِ فَهَذَا مَا لاَ اخْتِلاَفَ فِيهِ عِنْدَ أَحَدٍ مِنَ النَّاسِ وَلاَ بِبَلَدٍ مِنَ الْبُلْدَانِ فَبِأَىِّ شَىْءٍ أَخَذَ هَذَا أَوْ فِي أَىِّ مَوْضِعٍ مِنْ كِتَابِ اللَّهِ وَجَدَهُ فَإِنْ أَقَرَّ بِهَذَا فَلْيُقْرِرْ بِالْيَمِينِ مَعَ الشَّاهِدِ وَإِنْ لَمْ يَكُنْ ذَلِكَ فِي كِتَابِ اللَّهِ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ وَأَنَّهُ لَيَكْفِي مِنْ ذَلِكَ مَا مَضَى مِنَ السُّنَّةِ وَلَكِنِ الْمَرْءُ قَدْ يُحِبُّ أَنْ يَعْرِفَ وَجْهَ الصَّوَابِ وَمَوْقِعَ الْحُجَّةِ فَفِي هَذَا بَيَانُ مَا أَشْكَلَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ إِنْ شَاءَ اللَّهُ تَعَالَى ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Abu Salama ibn Abd ar- Rahman (successor); Sulayman ibn Yasar (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me that he heard that Abu Salama ibn Abd ar- Rahman and Sulayman ibn Yasar were both asked, &quot;Does one pronounce judgement on the basis of an oath with one witness?&quot; They both said, &quot;Yes.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1408]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1408" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1408</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1408 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The precedent of the sunna in judging by an oath with one witness is that if the plaintiff takes an oath with his witness, he is confirmed in his right. If he draws back and refuses to take an oath, the defendant is made to take an oath. If he takes an oath, the claim against him is dropped. If he refuses to take an oath, the claim is confirmed against him.&quot; Malik said, &quot;This procedure pertains to property cases in particular. It does not occur in any of the hadd-punishments, nor in marriage, divorce, freeing slaves, theft or slander. If some one says, &#x27;Freeing slaves comes under property,&#x27; he has erred. It is not as he said. Had it been as he said, a slave could take an oath with one witness, if he could find one, that his master had freed him. &quot;However, when a slave lays claim to a piece of property, he can take an oath with one witness and demand his right as the freeman demands his right.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The sunna with us is that when a slave brings somebody who witnesses that he has been set free, his master is made to take an oath that he has not freed him, and the slave&#x27;s claim is dropped.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The sunna about divorce is also like that with us. When a woman brings somebody who witnesses that her husband has divorced her, the husband is made to take an oath that he has not divorced her. If he takes the oath, the divorce does not proceed . &quot; Malik said, &quot;There is only one sunna of bringing a witness in cases of divorce and freeing a slave. The right to make an oath only belongs to the husband of the woman, and the master of the slave. Freeing is a hadd matter, and the testimony of women is not permitted in it because when a slave is freed, his inviolability is affirmed and the hadd punishments are applied for and against him. If he commits fornication and he is a muhsan, he is stoned. If he kills a slave, he is killed for it. Inheritance is established for him, between him and whoever inherits from him. If somebody disputes this, arguing that if a man frees his slave and then a man comes to demand from the master of the slave payment of a debt, and a man and two women testify to his right, that establishes the right against the master of the slave so that his freeing him is cancelled if he only has the slave as property, inferring by this case that the testimony of women is permitted in cases of setting free. The case is not as he suggests (i.e. it is a case of property not freeing). It is like a man who frees his slave, and then the claimant of a debt comes to the master and takes an oath with one witness, demanding his right. By that, the freeing of the slave would be cancelled. Or else a man comes who has frequent dealings and transactions with the master of the slave. He claims that he is owed money by the master of the slave. Someone says to the master of the slave, &#x27;Take an oath that you don&#x27;t owe what he claims&#x27;. If he draws back and refuses to take an oath, the one making the claim takes an oath and his right against the master of the slave is confirmed. That would cancel the freeing of the slave if it is confirmed that property is owed by the master.&quot; Malik said, &quot;It is the same case with a man who marries a slave-girl and then the master of the slave-girl comes to the man who has married her and claims, &#x27;You and so-and-so have bought my slave-girl from me for such an amount of dinars. The husband of the slave-girl denies that. The master of the slave-girl brings a man and two women and they testify to what he has said. The sale is confirmed and his claim is considered true. So the slave-girl is haram for her husband and they have to separate, even though the testimony of women is not accepted in divorce.&quot; Malik said, &quot;It is also the same case with a man who accuses a free man, so the hadd falls on him. A man and two women come and testify that the one accused is a slave. That would remove the hadd from the accused after it had befallen him, even though the testimony of women is not accepted in accusations involving hadd punishments.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Another similar case in which judgement appears to go against the precedent of the sunna is that two women testify that a child is born alive and so it is necessary for him to inherit if a situation arises where he is entitled to inherit, and the child&#x27;s property goes to those who inherit from him, if he dies, and it is not necessary that the two women witnesses should be accompanied by a man or an oath even though it may involve vast properties of gold, silver, live-stock, gardens and slaves and other properties. However, had two women testified to one dirham or more or less than that in a property case, their testimony would not affect anything and would not be permitted unless there was a witness or an oath with them.&quot; Malik said, &quot;There are people who say that an oath is not acceptable with only one witness and they argue by the word of Allah the Blessed, the Exalted, and His word is the Truth, &#x27;And call in to witness two witnesses, men; or if the two be not men, then one man and two women, such witnesses as you approve of.&#x27; (Sura 2 ayat 282). Such people argue that if he does not bring one man and two women, he has no claim and he is not allowed to take an oath with one witness.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Part of the proof against those who argue this, is to reply to them, &#x27;Do you think that if a man claimed property from a man, the one claimed from would not swear that the claim was false?&#x27; If he swears, the claim against him is dropped. If he refuses to take an oath, the claimant is made to take an oath that his claim is true, and his right against his companion is established. There is no dispute about this with any of the people nor in any country. By what does he take this? In what place in the Book of Allah does he find it? So if he confirms this, let him confirm the oath with one witness, even if it is not in the Book of Allah, the Mighty, the Majestic! It is enough that this is the precedent of the sunna. However, man wants to recognise the proper course of action and the location of the proof. In this there is a clarification for what is obscure about that, if Allah ta&#x27;ala wills</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">उमर बिन अब्दुल अजीज (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने अब्दुल हामिद बिन अब्दुल रहमान को लिखा, जो कूफा के आमिल (गवर्नर) थे, के एक कसम और एक गवाह पर फैसला किया करो। उम्म-ए-सलामा बिंत अब्दुर रहमान और सुलैमान बिन यासर से सवाल हुआ के क्या एक गवाह और एक कसम पर फैसला करना दुरुस्त है? उन्होंने कहा: हां। इमाम मालिक (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: जब मुद्दई (क्लेमेंट) के पास एक गवाह हो, तो उसकी गवाही लेकर मुद्दई को कसम देंगे, अगर वह कसम खा लेगा तो बरी हो जाएगा, और अगर वह कसम खाने से इनकार करे तो मुद्दई का दावा साबित हो जाएगा। इमाम मालिक (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: एक कसम और एक गवाह से फैसला सिर्फ अमवाल (माल-ओ-दौलत) के दावों में होगा, और हुदूद, निकाह, तलाक, इताक (गुलामी से आजादी), सरका (चोरी) और कज़फ़ में एक गवाह और एक कसम पर फैसला करना दुरुस्त नहीं। जिस शख्स ने इताक को अमवाल के दावों में शामिल किया, उसने गलती की। इसी तरह अगर औरत एक गवाह लाए के उसके शौहर ने तलाक दी है तो शौहर से कसम ली जाएगी। इमाम मालिक (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने मजीद तफसील बयान की के ये फैसला उसूल-ए-शरीयत के मुताबिक है और हदीस में मौजूद है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1408" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عمر بن عبدالعزیز نے لکھا عبدالحمید بن عبدالرحمن کو اور وہ عامل تھے کوفہ کے کہ ایک قسم اور ایک گواہ پر فیصلہ کیا کر ۔ ام سلمہ بن عبدالرحمن اور سلیمان بن یسار سے سوال ہوا کہ ایک گواہ اور ایک قسم پر فیصلہ کرنا درست ہے انہوں نے کہا ہاں ۔ کہا مالک نے جب مدعی کے پاس ایک گواہ ہو تو اس کی گواہی لے کر مدعی کو قسم دیں گے اگر وہ قسم کھالے گا تو بری ہو جائے گا اگر وہ قسم کھانے سے انکار کرے تو مدعی کا دعویٰ اس پر ثابت ہوجائے گا۔ کہا مالک نے ایک قسم اور ایک گواہ سے فیصلہ کرنا صرف اموال کے عدوے میں ہوگا اور حدود اور نکاح اور طلاق اور عتاق اور سرقہ اور قذف میں ایک گواہ اور ایک قسم پر فیصلہ کرنا درست نہیں اور جس شخص نے عتاق کو اموال کے دعوے میں داخل کیا اس نے غلطی کی کیونکہ اگر ایسا ہوتا تو غلام جب ایک گواہ لاتا اس امر پر کہ مولیٰ نے اس کو آزاد کر دیا ہے تو چاہیے تھا کہ غلام سے حلف لے کے اس کو آزاد کردیتے حالانکہ ایسا نہیں ہے بلکہ جب غلام اپنی آزادی پر ایک گواہ لائے تو اس کے مولیٰ سے حلف لیں گے اگر حلف کرلے گا تو آزادی ثابت نہ ہوگی۔ کہا مالک نے اسی طرح اگر عورت ایک گواہ لائے اس امر پر کہ اس کے خاوند نے اس کو طلاق دی تو خاوند سے قسم لیں گے اگر وہ قسم کھائے اس امر پر کہ میں نے طلاق نہیں دی تو طلاق ثابت نہ ہوگی۔ کہا مالک نے اگر طلاق اور عتاق میں جب ایک گواہ ہو تو خاوند اور مولیٰ پر قسم لازم آئے گی ۔ کیونکہ عتاق ایک حد شرعی ہے جس میں عورتوں کی گواہی درست نہیں اس لئے کہ غلام جب آزاد ہوجاتا ہے تو اس کی حرمت ثابت ہوجاتی ہے اور اس کی حدیں اوروں پر پڑتی ہیں اور اوروں کی حدیں اس پر پڑتی ہیں اگر وہ زنا کرے اور محصن ہو تو رجم کیا جائے گا اگر اس کو کوئی مار ڈالے تو قاتل بھی مارا جائے گا اور اس کے وارثوں کو میراث کا استحقاق حاصل ہوگا اگر کوئی حجت کرنے والا یہ کہے کہ مولیٰ جب غلام کو آزاد کر دے پھر ایک شخص اپنا قرض مولیٰ سے مانگنے آئے اور ایک مرد اور دو عورتوں کی گواہی سے اپنا قرض ثابت کرے تو مولیٰ پر قرضہ ثابت ہوجائے گا اگر مولیٰ کے پاس سوائے اس غلام کے کوئی مال نہ ہوگا تو اس غلام کی آزادی فسخ کر ڈالیں گے اس سے یہ بات نکالی کہ عورتوں کی گواہی عتاق میں درست ہے تو یہ نہیں ہوسکتا کیونکہ عورتوں کی گواہی قرضے کے اثبات میں معتبر ہوئی نہ کہ عتاق میں اس کی مثال یہ ہے کہ ایک شخص اپنے غلام کو آزاد کر دے پھر اس کا قرض خواہ ایک گواہ اور ایک قسم سے اپنا قرضہ مولیٰ پر ثابت کر دے اور اس کی وجہ سے آزادی فسخ کی جائے یا مولیٰ پر قرضے کا دعویٰ کرے اور گواہ نہ رکھتا ہو تو مولیٰ سے قسم لی جائے اور وہ انکار کرے تو مدعی سے قسم لے کر اس کا قرضہ ثابت کردیا جائے اور آزادی فسخ کی جائے اسی طرح ایک شخص نکاح کرے لونڈی سے پھر لونڈی کا مولیٰ خاوند سے کہنے لگے کہ تو نے اور فلاں شخص نے مل کر میری اس لونڈی کو اتنے دینار میں خرید کیا ہے اور خاوند انکار کرے تو مولیٰ ایک مرد اور دو عورتوں کو گواہ لائے اپنے قول پر اس صورت میں بیع ثابت ہوجائے گی ۔ اور وہ لونڈی خاوند پر حرام ہوجائے گی۔ اور نکاح فسخ ہوجائے گا حالانکہ طلاق میں عورتوں کی گواہی درست نہیں ۔ کہا مالک نے اسی طرح اگر ایک شخص قذف کرے ایک شخص کو پھر ایک مرد یا دو عورتیں گواہی دیں کہ جس شخص کو قذف کیا ہے وہ غلام ہے تو قاذف کے ذمہ سے حد ساقط ہوجائے گی حالانکہ قذف میں شہادت عورتوں کی درست نہیں ۔ کہا مالک نے یہ بھی اس کی مثال ہے کہ وہ عورتیں گواہی دیں بچے کے رونے پر تو اس بچے کے لئے میراث ثابت ہوجائے گی اور جو بچہ مر گیا ہوگا تو اس کے وارثوں کو میراث ملے گی حالانکہ ان دو عورتوں کے ساتھ نہ کوئی مرد ہے نہ قسم ہے اور کبھی میراث کا مال کثیر ہوتا ہے جیسے سونا چاندی زمین ، باغ، غلام وغیرہ اگر یہی دو عورتیں ایک درہم پر یا اس سے کم پر بھی گواہی دیں تو ان کی گواہی سے کچھ ثابت نہ ہوگا۔ جب تک کہ ان کے ساتھ ایک مرد یا ایک قسم نہ ہو۔ کہا مالک نے بعضے لوگ یہ کہتے ہیں کہ ایک قسم اور ایک گواہ سے حق ثابت نہیں ہو تو بہ سبب قول اللہ تعالیٰ کے فان لم یکونا رجلین الایة تو حجت ان لوگوں پر یہ ہے کہ آیا تم نہیں دیکھتے کہ اگر ایک شخص نے دعویٰ کیا ایک شخص پر مال کا کیا نہیں حلف لیا جاتا مدعی علیہ تو اگر حلف کرتا ہے باطل ہوجاتا ہے اس سے یہ یہ حق اگر نکول کرتا ہے پھر حلف دلاتے ہیں صاحب حق کو تو یہ امر ایسا ہے کہ نہیں ہے اختلاف اس میں کسی کا لوگوں میں سے اور نہ کسی شہر میں شہروں میں سے تو کسی دلیل سے نکالا ہے اس کو اور کس کتاب اللہ میں پایا ہے اس مسئلے کو تو جب اس امر کو اقرار کرے تو ضرور ہی اقرار کرے یمین مع الشاہد کا اگرچہ نہیں ہے یہ کتاب اللہ میں مگر حدیث میں تو موجود ہے آدمی کو چاہیے کہ ٹھیک راستہ پہچانے اور دلیل کا موقع دیکھے اس صورت میں اگر اللہ چاہے گا تو اس کی مشکل حل ہو جائے گی۔</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1408 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Umar bin Abdul Aziz (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne Abdul Hamid bin Abdul Rahman ko likha, jo Kufa ke aamil (governor) the, ke ek qasam aur ek gawah par faisla kiya karo. Umm-e-Salama bint Abdur Rahman aur Sulaiman bin Yasar se sawal hua ke kya ek gawah aur ek qasam par faisla karna durust hai? Unhone kaha: Haan. Imam Malik (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Jab mudda&#x27;i (claimant) ke paas ek gawah ho, toh uski gawahi lekar mudda&#x27;i ko qasam denge, agar woh qasam kha lega toh bari ho jayega, aur agar woh qasam khane se inkaar kare toh mudda&#x27;i ka da&#x27;wa sabit ho jayega. Imam Malik (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Ek qasam aur ek gawah se faisla sirf amwal (maal-o-daulat) ke da&#x27;won mein hoga, aur hudood, nikah, talaq, itaq (ghulami se azadi), sarqa (chori) aur qazf mein ek gawah aur ek qasam par faisla karna durust nahi. Jis shakhs ne itaq ko amwal ke da&#x27;won mein shamil kiya, usne ghalti ki. Isi tarah agar aurat ek gawah laye ke uske shauhar ne talaq di hai toh shauhar se qasam li jayegi. Imam Malik (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne mazid tafseel bayan ki ke ye faisla usool-e-shariat ke mutabiq hai aur hadith mein majood hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1408" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1394

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1409)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: عُمَرَ بْنَ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ (successor). Jamil ibn Abd al-Rahman narrates the judicial practice of Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz. This is a non-Prophetic historical report; Malik&#x27;s subsequent legal summary remains context.</p>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ يَحْيَى قَالَ مَالِكٌ عَنْ جَمِيلِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ الْمُؤَذِّنِ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَحْضُرُ عُمَرَ بْنَ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ وَهُوَ يَقْضِي بَيْنَ النَّاسِ فَإِذَا جَاءَهُ الرَّجُلُ يَدَّعِي عَلَى الرَّجُلِ حَقًّا نَظَرَ فَإِنْ كَانَتْ بَيْنَهُمَا مُخَالَطَةٌ أَوْ مُلاَبَسَةٌ أَحْلَفَ الَّذِي ادُّعِيَ عَلَيْهِ وَإِنْ لَمْ يَكُنْ شَىْءٌ مِنْ ذَلِكَ لَمْ يُحَلِّفْهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1409]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1409" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1409</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1409 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl"> قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَعَلَى ذَلِكَ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّهُ مَنِ ادَّعَى عَلَى رَجُلٍ بِدَعْوَى نُظِرَ فَإِنْ كَانَتْ بَيْنَهُمَا مُخَالَطَةٌ أَوْ مُلاَبَسَةٌ أُحْلِفَ الْمُدَّعَى عَلَيْهِ فَإِنْ حَلَفَ بَطَلَ ذَلِكَ الْحَقُّ عَنْهُ وَإِنْ أَبَى أَنْ يَحْلِفَ وَرَدَّ الْيَمِينَ عَلَى الْمُدَّعِي فَحَلَفَ طَالِبُ الْحَقِّ أَخَذَ حَقَّهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1409" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz (successor). Jamil ibn Abd ar-Rahman narrates Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz&#x27;s judicial practice. The preceding estate/debt analysis and subsequent compiler summary remain context; no Prophet attribution is asserted.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said, &quot;Malik said about Jamil ibn Abd ar-Rahman al-Muadhdin that he was present with Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz when he was judging between people. If a man came to him with a claim against a man, he examined whether or not there were frequent transactions and dealings between them. If there were, the defendant could make an oath. If there was nothing of that nature he did not accept an oath from him.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1409]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1409" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1409</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1409 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said that Malik spoke about a man who died and had a debt owing to him and there was one witness, and some people had a debt against him and they had only one witness, and his heirs refused to take an oath on their rights with their witness. He said, &quot;The creditors take an oath and take their rights. If there is anything left over, the heirs do not take any of it. That is because the oaths were offered to them before and they abandoned them, unless they say, &#x27;We did not know that our companion had extra,&#x27; and it is known that they only abandoned the oaths because of that. I think that they should take an oath and take what remains after his debt.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1409" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik summed up, &quot;What is done in our community is that if some one makes a claim against a man, it is examined. If there are frequent transactions and dealings between them, the defendant is made to take an oath. If he takes an oath, the claim against him is dropped. If the defendant refuses to take an oath, and returns the oath to the claimant, the one claiming his right takes an oath and takes his due</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1409" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">याह्या ने कहा के इमाम मलिक ने एक ऐसे आदमी के बारे में बात की जिस का इंतकाल हो गया और उस के जिम्मे क़र्ज़ था और सिर्फ एक गवाह मजूद था वरिषीन ने अपने गवाह के साथ क़सम खाने से इंकार कर दिया. उन्हों ने कहा: क़र्ज़ ख़्वाह क़सम खाएं और अपना हक़ ले लें. अगर कुछ बच जाये तो वरिषीन को इस में से कुछ नहीं मिलेगा क्यूंकि उन्हें पहले क़सम की पेशकश की गयी थी और उन्हों ने उसे छोड़ दिया था. अलबत्ता अगर वो कहें कि हमें मालूम नहीं था के हमारे साथी के पास कुछ ज़ैद है और ये मालूम हो जाये के उन्हों ने इसी वजह से क़सम छोड़ी थी तो मेरे ख्याल में उन्हें क़सम खा कर बाक़ी मन्दा हिस्सा ले लेना चाहिए.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1409" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے کہا کہ امام مالک نے ایک ایسے آدمی کے بارے میں بات کی جس کا انتقال ہو گیا اور اس کے ذمے قرض تھا اور صرف ایک گواہ موجود تھا، اسی طرح کچھ لوگوں کا اس پر قرض تھا اور ان کے پاس بھی صرف ایک گواہ تھا، اور اس کے ورثاء نے اپنے گواہ کے ساتھ قسم کھانے سے انکار کر دیا۔ انہوں نے کہا: قرض خواہ قسم کھائیں اور اپنا حق لے لیں۔ اگر کچھ بچ جائے تو ورثاء کو اس میں سے کچھ نہیں ملے گا کیونکہ انہیں پہلے قسم کی پیشکش کی گئی تھی اور انہوں نے اسے چھوڑ دیا تھا۔ البتہ اگر وہ کہیں کہ ہمیں معلوم نہیں تھا کہ ہمارے ساتھی کے پاس کچھ زائد ہے اور یہ معلوم ہو جائے کہ انہوں نے اسی وجہ سے قسم چھوڑی تھی تو میرے خیال میں انہیں قسم کھا کر باقی ماندہ حصہ لے لینا چاہیے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1409" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne kaha ke Imam Malik ne ek aise aadmi ke bare mein baat ki jis ka inteqal ho gaya aur us ke zimme qarz tha aur sirf ek gawah majood tha, isi tarah kuch logon ka us par qarz tha aur un ke paas bhi sirf ek gawah tha, aur us ke warisheen ne apne gawah ke sath qasam khane se inkar kar diya. Unhon ne kaha: Qarz khwah qasam khayein aur apna haq le lein. Agar kuch bach jaye to warisheen ko is mein se kuch nahi milega kyunke unhein pehle qasam ki peshkash ki gayi thi aur unhon ne use chhod diya tha. Albata agar wo kahein ke humein maloom nahi tha ke hamare sathi ke paas kuch zaid hai aur ye maloom ho jaye ke unhon ne isi wajah se qasam chhodi thi to mere khayal mein unhein qasam kha kar baqi manda hissa le lena chahiye.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1409" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1395

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1410)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1410]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1410" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1410</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1410 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ يَحْيَى قَالَ مَالِكٌ عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ الزُّبَيْرِ، كَانَ يَقْضِي بِشَهَادَةِ الصِّبْيَانِ فِيمَا بَيْنَهُمْ مِنَ الْجِرَاحِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ الْمُجْتَمَعُ عَلَيْهِ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ شَهَادَةَ الصِّبْيَانِ تَجُوزُ فِيمَا بَيْنَهُمْ مِنَ الْجِرَاحِ وَلاَ تَجُوزُ عَلَى غَيْرِهِمْ وَإِنَّمَا تَجُوزُ شَهَادَتُهُمْ فِيمَا بَيْنَهُمْ مِنَ الْجِرَاحِ وَحْدَهَا لاَ تَجُوزُ فِي غَيْرِ ذَلِكَ إِذَا كَانَ ذَلِكَ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَتَفَرَّقُوا أَوْ يُخَبَّبُوا أَوْ يُعَلَّمُوا فَإِنِ افْتَرَقُوا فَلاَ شَهَادَةَ لَهُمْ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَكُونُوا قَدْ أَشْهَدُوا الْعُدُولَ عَلَى شَهَادَتِهِمْ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَفْتَرِقُوا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1410" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn az-Zubayr (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said, &quot;Malik said from Hisham ibn Urwa that Abdullah ibn az-Zubayr gave judgment based on the testimony of children concerning the injuries between them.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1410]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1410" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1410</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1410 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things in our community is that the testimony of children is permitted concerning injuries between them. It is not accepted about anything else. It is only permitted between them if they testify before they leave the scene of the incident and have been deceived or instructed. If they leave the scene, they have no testimony unless they call just witnesses to witness their testimony before they leave</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1410" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1410]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1410" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1410</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1410 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">हिशाम बिन उरवा से रिवायत है के अब्दुल्लाह बिन जुबैर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) लड़कों की गवाही पर फैसला करते थे जो आपस में लड़-झगड़ जाते थे। इमाम मालिक (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: अगर लड़के लड़ कर एक दूसरे को ज़ख्मी करें तो उनकी गवाही दुरुस्त है, लेकिन लड़कों की गवाही और मुकद्दमात में दुरुस्त नहीं। ये तब दुरुस्त है जब वह लड़ कर फौरन वहां से अलग न हुए हों और मक्कारी न की हो। अगर जुदा जुदा हो गए हों तो फिर उनकी गवाही दुरुस्त नहीं, सिवाए इसके के उन्होंने आदिल (सच्चे) लोगों को अपनी शहादत पर गवाह बना लिया हो।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1410" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ہشام بن عروہ سے روایت ہے کہ عبداللہ بن زبیر لڑکوں کی گواہی پر حکم کرتے تھے ان کے آپس کی مار پیٹ کے ۔ کہا مالک نے لڑکے لڑ کر ایک دوسرے کو زخمی کریں تو ان کی گواہی درست ہے لیکن لڑکوں کی گواہی اور مقدمات میں درست نہیں ہے یہ بھی جب درست ہے کہ لڑ لڑا کر جدا نہ ہوگئے ہوں مکر نہ کیا ہو اگر جدا جدا چلے گئے ہوں تو پھر ان کی گواہی درست نہیں ہے مگر جب عادل لوگوں کو اپنی شہادت پر شاہد کر گئے ہوں ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1410" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Hisham bin Urwah se riwayat hai ke Abdullah bin Zubair (Radi Allahu Anhu) ladkon ki gawahi par faisla karte the jo aapas mein lad-jagad jate the. Imam Malik (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Agar ladke lad kar ek dusre ko zakhmi karein toh unki gawahi durust hai, lekin ladkon ki gawahi aur muqadmaat mein durust nahi. Ye tab durust hai jab woh lad kar fauran wahan se alag na huye hon aur makkari na ki ho. Agar juda juda ho gaye hon toh phir unki gawahi durust nahi, siwaye iske ke unhone aadil (sachche) logon ko apni shahadat par gawah bana liya ho.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1410" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1396

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1411)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ يَحْيَى حَدَّثَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنْ هَاشِمِ بْنِ هَاشِمِ بْنِ عُتْبَةَ بْنِ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ نِسْطَاسٍ، عَنْ جَابِرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ الأَنْصَارِيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَنْ حَلَفَ عَلَى مِنْبَرِي آثِمًا تَبَوَّأَ مَقْعَدَهُ مِنَ النَّارِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1411]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1411" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1411 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said, Malik related to us from Hisham ibn Hisham ibn Utba ibn Abi Waqqas from Abdullah ibn Nistas from Jabir ibn Abdullah al- Ansari that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;If someone swears a false oath near this mimbar of mine, he will take his seat in the fire</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1411]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1411" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">जाबिर बिन अब्दुल्लाह (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया जो शख्स मेरे मिंबर पर झूठी कसम खाए, उसने अपना ठिकाना बना लिया जहन्नुम में।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1411]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1411" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">

<p dir="rtl">جابر بن عبداللہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا جو شخص میرے منبر پر جھوٹی قسم کھائے اس نے اپنا ٹھکانہ نبالیا جہنم میں</p>

## Narration 1397

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1412)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنِ الْعَلاَءِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ مَعْبَدِ بْنِ كَعْبٍ السَّلَمِيِّ، عَنْ أَخِيهِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ كَعْبِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ الأَنْصَارِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِي أُمَامَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ مَنِ اقْتَطَعَ حَقَّ امْرِئٍ مُسْلِمٍ بِيَمِينِهِ حَرَّمَ اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ الْجَنَّةَ وَأَوْجَبَ لَهُ النَّارَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالُوا وَإِنْ كَانَ شَيْئًا يَسِيرًا يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ وَإِنْ كَانَ قَضِيبًا مِنْ أَرَاكٍ وَإِنْ كَانَ قَضِيبًا مِنْ أَرَاكٍ وَإِنْ كَانَ قَضِيبًا مِنْ أَرَاكٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَهَا ثَلاَثَ مَرَّاتٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1412]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1412" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1412</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1412 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from al-Ala ibn Abd ar-Rahman from Mabad ibn Kab as-Salami from his brother Abdullah ibn Kab ibn Malik al-Ansari from Abu Umama that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Whoever cuts off the right of a muslim man by his oath, Allah forbids him the Garden and obliges the Fire for him.&quot; They said, &quot;Even if it is something insignificant, Messenger of Allah?&quot; He said, &quot;Even if it is a tooth-stick, even if it is a tooth- stick,&quot; repeating it three times</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1412]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1412" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1412</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1412 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक ने मुझे अल-अला इब्न अब्द अर-रहमान से, उन्होंने मबाद इब्न काब अस-सलमी से, उन्होंने अपने भाई अब्दुल्लाह इब्न काब इब्न मालिक अल-अंसारी से, उन्होंने अबू उमामा से रिवायत की के अल्लाह के रसूल (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: जो शख्स अपनी कसम के ज़रिए किसी मुसलमान का हक मार ले, अल्लाह उस पर जन्नत हराम कर देता है और जहन्नुम वाजिब कर देता है। उन्होंने अर्ज़ किया: ऐ अल्लाह के रसूल! अगरचे वो कोई मामूली चीज़ ही क्यों न हो? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: अगरचे वो मिसवाक ही क्यों न हो, अगरचे वो मिसवाक ही क्यों न हो (आप ने तीन बार फरमाया)।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1412]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1412" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک نے مجھے العلاء بن عبدالرحمن سے، انہوں نے معبد بن کعب سلمیٰ سے، انہوں نے اپنے بھائی عبداللہ بن کعب بن مالک انصاری سے، انہوں نے ابو امامہ سے روایت کی کہ اللہ کے رسول صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: جو شخص اپنی قسم کے ذریعے کسی مسلمان کا حق مار لے، اللہ اس پر جنت حرام کر دیتا ہے اور جہنم واجب کر دیتا ہے۔ انہوں نے عرض کیا: اے اللہ کے رسول! اگرچہ وہ کوئی معمولی چیز ہی کیوں نہ ہو؟ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: اگرچہ وہ مسواک ہی کیوں نہ ہو، اگرچہ وہ مسواک ہی کیوں نہ ہو (آپ نے تین بار فرمایا)۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1412]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1412" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ne mujhe al-Ala ibn Abd ar-Rahman se, unhon ne Mabad ibn Kab as-Salami se, unhon ne apne bhai Abdullah ibn Kab ibn Malik al-Ansari se, unhon ne Abu Umama se riwayat ki ke Allah ke Rasool (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Jo shakhs apni qasam ke zariye kisi musalman ka haq maar le, Allah us par jannat haram kar deta hai aur jahannum wajib kar deta hai. Unhon ne arz kiya: Aye Allah ke Rasool! Agarcha wo koi mamooli cheez hi kyun na ho? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Agarcha wo miswak hi kyun na ho, agarcha wo miswak hi kyun na ho (aap ne teen bar farmaya).</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1412]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1412" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1398

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1413)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1413]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1413" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1413</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1413 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ يَحْيَى قَالَ مَالِكٌ عَنْ دَاوُدَ بْنِ الْحُصَيْنِ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ أَبَا غَطَفَانَ بْنَ طَرِيفٍ الْمُرِّيَّ، يَقُولُ اخْتَصَمَ زَيْدُ بْنُ ثَابِتٍ الأَنْصَارِيُّ وَابْنُ مُطِيعٍ فِي دَارٍ كَانَتْ بَيْنَهُمَا إِلَى مَرْوَانَ بْنِ الْحَكَمِ وَهُوَ أَمِيرٌ عَلَى الْمَدِينَةِ فَقَضَى مَرْوَانُ عَلَى زَيْدِ بْنِ ثَابِتٍ بِالْيَمِينِ عَلَى الْمِنْبَرِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ زَيْدُ بْنُ ثَابِتٍ أَحْلِفُ لَهُ مَكَانِي ‏.‏ قَالَ فَقَالَ مَرْوَانُ لاَ وَاللَّهِ إِلاَّ عِنْدَ مَقَاطِعِ الْحُقُوقِ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَجَعَلَ زَيْدُ بْنُ ثَابِتٍ يَحْلِفُ أَنَّ حَقَّهُ لَحَقٌّ ‏.‏ وَيَأْبَى أَنْ يَحْلِفَ عَلَى الْمِنْبَرِ - قَالَ - فَجَعَلَ مَرْوَانُ بْنُ الْحَكَمِ يَعْجَبُ مِنْ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ لاَ أَرَى أَنْ يُحَلَّفَ أَحَدٌ عَلَى الْمِنْبَرِ عَلَى أَقَلَّ مِنْ رُبُعِ دِينَارٍ وَذَلِكَ ثَلاَثَةُ دَرَاهِمَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1413" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This reading preserves separate source spans. Intervening source notes are retained under Comments. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Zayd ibn Thabit al-Ansari (companion). The oath dispute remains one exact report in two passages around an explanatory parenthesis. Subsequent compiler opinion remains context. Some reports contain separate source passages. Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said that Malik had said from Da&#x27;ud ibn al-Husayn that he heard Abu Ghatafan ibn Tarif al-Muriyi say, &quot;Zayd ibn Thabit al-Ansari and Ibn Muti had a dispute about a house which they shared. They went to Marwan ibn al-Hakam who was the Amir of Madina. Marwan decided that Zayd ibn Thabit must take an oath on the mimbar. Zayd ibn Thabit said, &#x27;I swear to it where I am.&#x27; Marwan said, &#x27;No, by Allah! only in the place of sorting out claims</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr">.&#x27; Zayd ibn Thabit began to take an oath that his right was true, and he refused to take an oath near the mimbar. Marwan ibn al-Hakam began to wonder at that.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1413]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1413" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1413 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> (i.e. the mimbar)</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1413" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;I do not think that anyone should be made to take an oath near the mimbar for less than a fourth of a dinar, and that is three dirhams</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अबी गफ्फान बिन तारीफ से रिवायत है के ज़ैद बिन साबित और अब्दुल्लाह बिन मुती ने झगड़ा किया एक घर में जो दोनों में मुश्तरक था, तो वह मुकद्दमा मरवान बिन हकम के पास ले गए क्योंकि वह उन दिनों में मदीने का हाकिम था। मरवान ने फैसला किया के ज़ैद बिन साबित मिंबर-ए-शरीफ पर कसम खाएं। ज़ैद ने कहा मैं अपनी जगह पर कसम खाऊंगा। मरवान ने कहा नहीं, वहीं कसम खाओ जहां लोग कज़िए चुकाते हैं। ज़ैद बिन साबित कसम खाते थे के मैं सच्चा हूं लेकिन मिंबर पर कसम खाने से इनकार करते थे और मरवान को इस पर ताज्जुब होता था।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1413" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ابی غطفان بن طریف سے روایت ہے کہ زید بن ثابت اور عبداللہ بن مطیع نے جھگڑا کیا ایک گھر میں جو دنوں میں مشترک تھا تو لے گئے مقدمہ مروان بن حکم کے پاس وہ ان دنوں میں حاکم تھا مدینہ کا مروانے فیصلہ کیا اس بات پر کہ زید بن ثابت قسم کھائیں منبر شریف پر زید نے کہا میں اپنی جگہ پر قسم کھاؤں گا مروان نے کہا نہیں وہیں قسم کھاؤ جہاں لوگوں نے قضیے چکتے ہیں تو زید بن ثابت قسم کھاتے تھے میں سچاہوں لیکن منبر پر قسم کھانے سے انکار کرتے تھے اور مروان کو تعجب ہوتا تھا۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abi Ghaffan bin Tarif se riwayat hai ke Zaid bin Thabit aur Abdullah bin Mut&#x27;i ne jhagda kiya ek ghar mein jo dono mein mushtarak tha, toh woh muqadma Marwan bin Hakam ke paas le gaye kyunke woh un dino mein Madine ka hakim tha. Marwan ne faisla kiya ke Zaid bin Thabit mimbar-e-sharif par qasam khayein. Zaid ne kaha main apni jagah par qasam khaunga. Marwan ne kaha nahi, wahin qasam khao jahan log qaziye chukate hain. Zaid bin Thabit qasam khate the ke main sacha hoon lekin mimbar par qasam khane se inkar karte the aur Marwan ko is par ta&#x27;ajjub hota tha.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1413" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1399

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1414)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ يَحْيَى حَدَّثَنَا مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يَغْلَقُ الرَّهْنُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1414]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1414" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said, &quot;Malik related to us from Ibn Shihab from Sa&#x27;id ibn al-Musayyab that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;The pledge given as security is not forfeited.&#x27; &quot; Malik said, &quot;The explanation of that according to what we think - and Allah knows best - is that a man gives a pledge to somebody in security for something. The pledge is superior to that for which he pawned it. The pledger says to the pawn-broker, &#x27;I will bring you your due, after such-and-such a time. If not, the pledge is yours for what it was pawned for.&#x27; &quot; Malik said, &quot;This transaction is not good and it is not halal. This is what was forbidden. If the owner brings what he pledged it for after the period, it is his. I think that the time condition is void</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1414]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1414" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने कहा के इमाम मालिक ने हमें इब्न शिहाब से, उन्होंने सईद इब्न अल-मुसय्यब से रिवायत की के अल्लाह के रसूल (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: गिरवी रखी हुई चीज़ ज़ाया नहीं होती। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: इस की वज़ाहत जो हमारे नज़दीक है, और अल्लाह बेहतर जानता है, ये है के एक आदमी किसी चीज़ के बदले किसी को गिरवी देता है और कहता है के अगर मैं ने इतनी मुद्दत में तुम्हारा हक़ अदा न किया तो ये गिरवी तुम्हारी हो जाए गी। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: ये मामला दुरुस्त नहीं और हलाल नहीं, यही वो चीज़ है जिस से मना किया गया था।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1414]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1414" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے کہا کہ امام مالک نے ہمیں ابن شہاب سے، انہوں نے سعید بن مسیب سے روایت کی کہ اللہ کے رسول صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: گروی رکھی ہوئی چیز ضائع نہیں ہوتی۔ امام مالک نے کہا: اس کی وضاحت جو ہمارے نزدیک ہے، اور اللہ بہتر جانتا ہے، یہ ہے کہ ایک آدمی کسی چیز کے بدلے کسی کو گروی دیتا ہے اور کہتا ہے کہ اگر میں نے اتنی مدت میں تمہارا حق ادا نہ کیا تو یہ گروی تمہاری ہو جائے گی۔ امام مالک نے کہا: یہ معاملہ درست نہیں اور حلال نہیں، یہی وہ چیز ہے جس سے منع کیا گیا تھا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1414]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne kaha ke Imam Malik ne hamain Ibn Shihab se, unhon ne Saeed ibn al-Musayyab se riwayat ki ke Allah ke Rasool (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Girwi rakhi hui cheez zaya nahi hoti. Imam Malik ne kaha: Is ki wazahat jo hamare nazdeek hai, aur Allah behtar janta hai, ye hai ke ek aadmi kisi cheez ke badle kisi ko girwi deta hai aur kehta hai ke agar main ne itni muddat mein tumhara haq ada na kiya to ye girwi tumhari ho jaye gi. Imam Malik ne kaha: Ye mamla durust nahi aur halal nahi, yahi wo cheez hai jis se mana kiya gaya tha.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1414]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1414" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1400

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 36</strong></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ الْمَلِكِ بْنَ مَرْوَانَ، قَضَى فِي امْرَأَةٍ أُصِيبَتْ مُسْتَكْرَهَةً بِصَدَاقِهَا عَلَى مَنْ فَعَلَ ذَلِكَ بِهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى سَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا فِي الرَّجُلِ يَغْتَصِبُ الْمَرْأَةَ بِكْرًا كَانَتْ أَوْ ثَيِّبًا إِنَّهَا إِنْ كَانَتْ حُرَّةً فَعَلَيْهِ صَدَاقُ مِثْلِهَا وَإِنْ كَانَتْ أَمَةً فَعَلَيْهِ مَا نَقَصَ مِنْ ثَمَنِهَا وَالْعُقُوبَةُ فِي ذَلِكَ عَلَى الْمُغْتَصِبِ وَلاَ عُقُوبَةَ عَلَى الْمُغْتَصَبَةِ فِي ذَلِكَ كُلِّهِ وَإِنْ كَانَ الْمُغْتَصِبُ عَبْدًا فَذَلِكَ عَلَى سَيِّدِهِ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَشَاءَ أَنْ يُسَلِّمَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1415" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Exact English field independently read in full. The selected passage is a locally explicit Prophetic report. Surrounding companion, jurist, compiler and explanatory source material remains under Comments. No source wording is altered and no authenticity or translation-equivalence judgment is made.</p>

<p dir="ltr">The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;If someone sells a palm which has been pollinated, the fruit belongs to the seller unless the buyer stipulates its inclusion.&#x27;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1415]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1415" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1415</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1415 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said, &quot;I heard Malik say that if a man pledges his garden for a stated period and the fruits of that garden are ready before the end of that period, the fruits are not included in the pledge with the real estate, unless it is stipulated by the pledger in his pledge. However, if a man receives a slave-girl as a pledge and she is pregnant or she becomes pregnant after his taking her as a pledge, her child is included with her. &quot;A distinction is made between the fruit and the child of the slave-girl. </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1415" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> The undisputed way of doing things in our community is that if a man sells a slave-girl or an animal with a foetus in its womb, the foetus belongs to the buyer, whether or not the buyer stipulates it. The palm is not like the animal. Fruit is not like the foetus in its mother&#x27;s womb. Part of what clarifies that is also that it is the usage of people to have a man pawn the fruit of the palm apart from the palm. No one pawns the foetus in its mother&#x27;s womb whether of slaves or animals.&quot; Yahya said that he had heard Malik say, &quot;The undisputed way of doing things in our community concerning pledges is that in cases where land or a house or an animal are known to have been destroyed whilst in the possession of the broker of the pledge, and the circumstances of the loss are known, the loss is against the pledger. There is no deduction made from what is due to the broker at all. Any pledge which perishes in the possession of the broker and the circumstances of its loss are only known by his word, the loss is against the broker and he is liable for its value. He is asked to describe whatever was destroyed and then he is made to take an oath about that description and what he loaned on security for it. &quot;Then people of discernment evaluate the description. If the pledge was worth more than what the broker loaned, the pledger takes the extra. If the assessed value of the pledge is less than what he was loaned, the pledger is made to take an oath as to what the broker loaned and he does not have to pay the extra which the broker loaned above the assessed value of the pledge. If the pledger refuses to take an oath, he has to give the broker the extra above the assessed value of the pledge. If the broker says that he doesn&#x27;t know the value of the pledge, the pledger is made to take an oath on the description of the pledge and that is his if he brings a matter which is not disapproved of.&quot; Malik said, &quot;All this applies when the broker takes the pledge and does not put it in the hands of another.&quot; Yahya said that he heard Malik speak about two men who had a pledge between them. One of them undertook to sell his pledge, and the other one had asked him to wait a year for his due. He said, &quot;If it is possible to divide the pledge, and the due of the one who asked him to wait will not be decreased, half the pledge which is between them is sold for him and he is given his due. If it is feared that his right will be decreased, all the pledge is sold, and the one who undertook to sell his pledge is given his due from that. If the one who asked him to wait for his due is pleased in himself, half of the price is paid to the pledger. If not, the pledgee is made to take an oath that he only asked him to wait so that he could transfer my pledge to me in its form.&#x27; Then he is given his due immediately.&quot; Yahya said that he heard Malik say about a slave whose master had pledged him and the slave had property of his own, &quot;The property of the slave is not part of the pledge unless the broker stipulates that.&quot; Yahya said that he heard Malik speak about someone who pledged goods as security for a loan, and they perished with the broker. The one who took out the loan confirmed its specification. They agreed on the amount of the loan, but challenged each other about the value of the pledge, the pledger saying that it had been worth twenty dinars, whilst the broker said that it had been worth only ten, and that the amount loaned on security was twenty dinars. Malik said, &quot;It is said to the one in whose hand the pledge is, &#x27;describe it.&#x27; If he describes it he is made to take an oath on it and then the people of experience evaluate that description. If the value is more than what was loaned on security for it, it is said to the broker, &#x27;Return the rest of his due to the pledger.&#x27; If the value is less than what was loaned on security for it, the broker takes the rest of his due from the pledger. If the value is the exact amount of the loan, the pledge is compensated for by the loan.&quot; Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;What is done in our community about two men who have a dispute about an amount of money loaned on the security of a pledge - the pledger claiming that he pledged it for ten dinars and the broker insisting that he took the pledge as security for twenty dinars, and the pledge is clearly in the possession of the broker - is that the broker is made to take an oath when the value of the pledge is fully known. If the value of the pledge is exactly what he swore that he had loaned on security for it, the broker takes the pledge as his right. He is more entitled to take precedence with an oath since he has possession of the pledge. If the owner of the pledge wants to give him the amount which he swore that he was owed, he can take the pledge back. If the pledge is worth less than the twenty dinars he loaned, then it is said to the pledger, &#x27;Either you give him what he has sworn to and take your pledge back, or you swear to what you said you pledged it for.&#x27; If the pledger takes the oath, then what the broker has increased over the value of the pledge will become invalid. If the pledger does not take an oath, he must pay what the broker swore to.&quot; Malik said, &quot;If a pledge given on security for a loan perishes, and both parties deny each other&#x27;s rights, with the broker who is owed the loan saying that he gave twenty dinars, and the pledger who owes the loan saying that he was given only ten, and with the broker who is owed the loan saying the pledge was worth ten dinars, and the broker who owes the loan saying it was worth twenty, then the broker who is owed the loan is asked to describe the pledge. If he describes it, he must take an oath on its description. Then people with experience of it evaluate that description. If the value of the pledge is estimated to be more than what the broker claims it was, he takes an oath as to what he claimed, and the pledger is given what is over from the value of the pledge. If its value is less than what the broker claims of it, he is made to take an oath as to what he claims is his. Then he demands settlement according to the actual value of the pledge. The one who owes the loan is then made to take an oath on the extra amount which remains owing against him to the claimant after the price of the pledge is reached. That is because the broker becomes a claimant against the pledger. If he takes an oath, the rest of what the broker swore to of what he claimed above the value of the pledge is invalidated. If he draws back, he is bound to pay what remains due to the broker after the value of the pledge.&quot; Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;What is done in our community about a man who rents an animal for a journey to a specified place and then he goes beyond that place and further, is that the owner of the animal has a choice. If he wants to take extra rent for his animal to cover the distance overstepped, he is given that on top of the first rent and the animal is returned. If the owner of the animal likes to sell the animal from the place where he over-steps, he has the price of the animal on top of the rent. If, however, the hirer rented the animal to go and return and then he overstepped when he reached the city to which he rented him, the owner of the animal only has half the first rent. That is because half of the rent is going, and half of it is returning. If he oversteps with the animal, only half of the first rent is obliged for him. Had the animal died when he reached the city to which it was rented, the hirer would not be liable and the renter would only have half the rent.&quot; Malik said, &quot;That is what is done with people who overstep and dispute about what they took the animal for.&quot; Malik said, &quot;It is also like that with some one who takes qirad-money from his companion. The owner of the property says to him, &#x27;Do not buy such-and-such animals or such- and-such goods.&#x27; He names them and forbids them and disapproves of his money being invested in them. The one who takes the money then buys what he was forbidden. By that, he intends to be liable for the money and take the profit of his companion. When he does that, the owner of the money has an option. If he wants to enter with him in the goods according to the original stipulations between them about the profit, he does so. If he likes, he has his capital guaranteed against the one who took the capital and over stepped the mark.&quot; Malik said, &quot;It is also like that with a man with whom another man invests some goods. The owner of the property orders him to buy certain goods for him which he names. He differs, and buys with the goods something other than what he was ordered to buy. He exceeded his orders. The owner of the goods has an option. If he wants to take what was bought with his property, he takes it. If he wants the partner to be liable for his capital he has that.&quot; Malik related to me from Ibn Shihab that Abd al-Malik ibn Marwan gave a judgment that the rapist had to pay the raped woman her bride- price. Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;What is done in our community about the man who rapes a woman, virgin or non-virgin, if she is free, is that he must pay the bride-price of the like of her. If she is a slave, he must pay what he has diminished of her worth. The hadd-punishment in such cases is applied to the rapist, and there is no punishment applied to the raped woman. If the rapist is a slave, that is against his master unless he wishes to surrender him</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1415" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मलिक ने फ़रमाया के जिस तरह बाघ गिरवी रखा जाये तो उस के फल तब तक गिरवी में शामिल नहीं होते जब तक शर्त न रखी जाये वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: अगर कोई शख़्स खजूर का दरख़्त बेचे जिस को पैवन्द लगा दिया गया हो तो फल बेचने वाले का है सिवाए इस के के खरीदार शर्त लगाए. हमारे मुआशरे में तरीक़ा ये है के अगर कोई घुलम या जानवर फरोख्त किया जाये जिस के पेट में बच्चा हो तो वो ख़रीदार का होता है.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1415" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک نے فرمایا کہ جس طرح باغ گروی رکھا جائے تو اس کے پھل تب تک گروی میں شامل نہیں ہوتے جب تک شرط نہ رکھی جائے، جبکہ لونڈی حاملہ ہو تو بچہ اس کے ساتھ شامل ہوتا ہے۔ اللہ کے رسول صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: اگر کوئی شخص کھجور کا درخت بیچے جس کو پیوند لگا دیا گیا ہو تو پھل بیچنے والے کا ہے سوائے اس کے کہ خریدار شرط لگائے۔ ہمارے معاشرے میں طریقہ یہ ہے کہ اگر کوئی غلام یا جانور فروخت کیا جائے جس کے پیٹ میں بچہ ہو تو وہ خریدار کا ہوتا ہے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1415" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1415]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1415" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1415</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1415 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ne farmaya ke jis tarah baagh girwi rakha jaye to us ke phal tab tak girwi mein shamil nahi hote jab tak shart na rakhi jaye, jabke laundi hamila ho to bacha us ke sath shamil hota hai. Allah ke Rasool (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Agar koi shakhs khajoor ka darakht beche jis ko paiwand laga diya gaya ho to phal bechne wale ka hai siwaye is ke ke khareedar shart lagaye. Hamare muashre mein tariqa ye hai ke agar koi ghulam ya janwar farokht kiya jaye jis ke pait mein bacha ho to wo khareedar ka hota hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1415" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>
